I used to take a lot of pride in my cooking. But when I get like that... I barely even taste the food. So... yeah. Pretty fast.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you're not in control of your eating during the day?\n\nPatient: No, not really. During the day, it's... fine. I just eat normal meals. It's really just the night when things get out of hand.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your weight over time because of these late-night eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. \n\nI mean, I haven't really been paying attention to that. So... I'm not sure. I don't think there's been any big changes. Just... normal, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel stressed, anxious, or upset before these late-night eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know. I don't really pay attention to that. \n\nI wouldn't say I'm... upset or anything. I just... I don't really think about how I'm feeling right before. It just sort of happens.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your late-night eating episodes are triggered by feelings of boredom?\n\nPatient: Um... no. I don't think so. \n\nI wouldn't say I'm... bored. I just... I don't really know why it starts. Like I said, it just kind of happens. I don't really think about it.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're unable to stop eating once you've started during these late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I guess so. \n\nIt's just... once I start, it's like I can't really pull back. I'll take a bite of something, and then... it's like my body just takes over. And I'll eat way past a normal plate. Like... if there are event leftovers in there, I'll just eat them. Fried chicken, mac and cheese, sweet potatoes, rolls... I don't really stop until a whole tray is empty, or... half the fridge is just gone. So, yeah. Not really. \n\nIt's... well, once I take that first bite, it just feels like the decision has already been made for me. I'll be standing there, telling myself George, stop now, but... my hands just keep fixing another plate. Or opening another container. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel guilty after these late-night eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Yeah. I do. \n\nUm... it's hard to talk about. Afterward, I just feel sick with myself. It's... people look at me and they see this organized, strong guy, you know? But then I'm just sitting there in the kitchen... ashamed. Just really guilty and disgusted that I let it happen again. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel hungry before you start eating at night?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not usually. \n\nActually, a lot of the time I'm not hungry at all. I mean... I might have just eaten dinner with my wife. But later on, I still go back in there. I just... start picking at the leftovers. And then it just turns into... another full-on spread. So... yeah. Not really hungry.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever have these episodes of eating a large amount of food in a short time during the day, not just at night?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. \n\nIt's pretty much... just a night thing. During the day, I don't really... it just doesn't happen like that. So... yeah. Just at night.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're eating really fast during these late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. I guess I do. \n\nI don't really sit down or take my time. I'm usually just... standing there at the counter. And I'm just taking these big bites. Half the time, I'm swallowing before I've even finished chewing the last one. \n\nIt's... I don't know. I used to take a lot of pride in my cooking. But when I get like that... I barely even taste the food. So... yeah. Pretty fast.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you're not in control of your eating during the day?\n\nPatient: No, not really. During the day, it's... fine. I just eat normal meals. It's really just the night when things get out of hand.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your weight over time because of these late-night eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Just... normal, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel stressed, anxious, or upset before these late-night eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't know. I don't really pay attention to that. \n\nI wouldn't say I'm... upset or anything. I just... I don't really think about how I'm feeling right before. I just... I don't really know why it starts. Like I said, it just kind of happens. I don't really think about it.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're unable to stop eating once you've started during these late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I'll be standing there, thinking to myself, 'George, you need to stop,' but... I just keep getting more food out. I just... I can't seem to make myself stop.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually try to hide or keep secret the fact that you're eating at night?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. \n\nI mean... it's late at night, so... I guess there's no one really around anyway. But I don't... I wouldn't say I'm trying to keep it a secret or anything. I'm just... I'm just in the kitchen.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel guilty because you feel like you've lost control while eating, rather than just because of the amount of food you've eaten?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I suppose. \n\nIt's... it's mostly just... afterward, I feel sick with myself. Um... people see me as this... organized, strong guy, I guess. But then I'm just sitting there... ashamed. Guilty. Just... disgusted that I let it happen again.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you're eating at night because you're feeling emotional?\n\nPatient: I don't know. Maybe. \n\nI don't really... think about why I'm doing it. I just do it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your eating at night helps you feel better when you're feeling emotional?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. \n\nI don't... I don't feel better. Like I just said, I usually feel worse after. So... no, I wouldn't say it helps.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you're eating at night as a way to deal with or escape from feelings or thoughts that are bothering you?\n\nPatient: I'm not sure. I can't seem to make myself stop.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually try to hide or keep secret the fact that you're eating at night?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. \n\nI mean... it's late at night, so...
